...RESPONDENTS
Petition under Article 226 of lhe constitution of lndia praying that in the
circumstances stated in the affidavit filed therewith, the High court may be
pleased to issue an appropriate writ, order or direction, more particularly one in
the nature of writ of Mandamus declaring the action of the Respondents in
filling up 359 posts of Supervisor Grade-[[ in Hyderabad Region of
lntegrated child Development services in the women Development and
child welfare Department through Notification No.22812013 d1.2.7.2013
without regularizing the services of the Petitioners, who are working as
Supervisor Grade-ll on contract basis for the last 15 to 20 years having
selected and appointed as such in regular selection process as illegal'
unjust, arbitrary, discriminatory, suffers from total non-application of mind
and violative of Articles 14, 16,21 & 311 0f Constitution of lndia and
consequently direct the Respondents not to fill up the posts of supervisor
Grade-ll held by the Petitioners though the impugned Notification while
directing the Respondents to regularized the services of the Petitioner's as
supervisor Grade-ll from the date of their initial appointment i.e., in the
year 2003 & 2006 as the case may be with all benefits, including notional
senioritY fixation of PaY scale' (}
